﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿6. The Sidrat Boundary

T﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍﻿he architecture has a hard boundary. The name comes from the program’s theological grounding: the Lote
